**Parallel execution order.** Five workstreams share the existing four service lanes: source/crosswalk and independent review (LIR-02/05/06), complete IR and scoped graph (LIR-04/07–11), model/evaluation preparation (LIR-17–20/23), Lean/invalidation (LIR-12–15), and supervisor/repair/release control (LIR-01/03/21/22/24). Schedule disjoint files/dependency components with existing claims and exclusive keys; reserve independent verifier roles. Preparation may advance in parallel, while dependency acceptance gates stay fixed. Every runnable child binds its existing goal/task CID, current plan/tree/revision, dependency outputs, exact inputs/source/codec/profile, argv, outputs, time/CPU/memory/storage/child/retry budget and verifier. Missing inputs are blocked, since runtime `proposed` can be selectable.

### Executable children and parallel work within the existing programme

The goal remains a versioned source-to-IR-to-scoped-graph-to-Lean4 corpus for the
entire selected U.S. Code release. It is a delivery goal, not an achieved corpus
or legal-fidelity claim. Keep the original seven phases and 24 task records above.
The [fresh bounded source recheck](../../../../workspace/test-logs/federal-corpus-audits/legal-ir-programme-review-20261022-05/supervisor-planning-watchdog-v01/hub-source-recheck-v01.json)
retains the same three selected Hub revisions; it downloads no dataset bodies and
selects no governing edition, legal-as-of date or source meaning.
The retained 135/135 legacy engineering-board completion records describe their
capture/replay tasks; they do not complete those 24 scientific tasks or create
their next executable population. Neither document is a current fleet-liveness
observation.

The [bounded child-task dispatch preview](../../../../workspace/test-logs/federal-corpus-audits/legal-ir-programme-review-20261022-05/supervisor-planning-watchdog-v01/child-task-dispatch-contract-v02.json)
maps existing parent IDs to technical children and blocked scientific follow-ons.
Its status is `documentation_only_not_registered`, with `execution_ready=false`
and null live CID/plan/tree/claim handles. It is one planning input, not a new
board, owner, admitted task population or runtime authority. Reuse the existing
`IntentRepository` and `DatabaseTaskSource` in `external/ipfs_accelerate` when
materializing a selected bounded population through its current owner.

Each admitted child must bind its persisted objective, goal/task canonical CIDs,
selected plan and task-tree/source-revision context, expected current revision,
dependency task CIDs, exact source/profile/input references, declared outputs,
acceptance and validation evidence, and finite resource/source-custody contract.
Retain the parent LIR ID and descriptive alias for navigation; aliases and JSON
files cannot substitute for live identities or claims. Reuse the current task,
claim, expected-revision and completion-evidence transitions rather than writing
projection tables or setting a parent's scientific status from a child result.

Documentary `proposed` means a planning candidate. The existing typed repository
includes runtime `proposed` among ready-selectable statuses: it is not a safe
placeholder for missing prerequisites. A newly admitted task whose scientific
inputs or execution authority are missing must start `blocked` with explicit
activation requirements, and transition only through its existing owner after
those requirements are witnessed. Unregistered preview entries have no actual
queue status. Edition, source meaning, independent context permission and
every-use applicability remain unprovided where the retained evidence says so;
a watchdog restart or a worker confidence score cannot supply them.

| E — supervisor operations | LIR-01,03,23 | Inspect the exact selected units, source generation and persisted task/claim population. Reuse bounded recovery and resource owners; distinguish genuine idle-complete state and scientific blocking from operational stalls. Deployment observations retain their own receipt below or in the selected catalog. |

Source review, engineering preparation and selected compatibility work may run
in parallel, subject to the original dependency contracts, source leases and
resource admission. Completed child outputs satisfy only their declared gates;
they do not remove scientific prerequisites from dependent tasks. Preserve every
failed attempt, receipt and independently qualified generation.

For whole-code expansion, first complete the norm/definition/two-use component,
then the original 20–40-provision pilot, then budgeted dependency components under
LIR-21–24. Bind the source release and classify operative provisions, headings,
notes and duplicate physical occurrences before enrollment. Reuse the existing
coverage funnel: `N_enrolled`, `N_reviewed`, `N_representable`, `N_attempted`,
`N_complete`, `N_exact`, `N_meaning_reviewed`, `N_graph_closed` and
`N_kernel_checked`. Report actual counts and explicit unsupported, ambiguous,
excluded and unresolved dispositions; mixed index rows and completed engine
tasks are not formalized-law counts. No whole-U.S.-Code or convergence claim
follows from the current fixtures, preparation or authored Lean builds.

#### Persistent watchdog and scoped recovery, 2026-10-26

The [plan and operational delivery record](../../../../docs/autoformalization/evidence/legal-ir-supervisor-plan-watchdog-20261026.json)
binds the current source review, child preview, separate regression cohorts and
actual scoped watchdog deployment/readback. The enabled LegalIR workers target
starts the four existing lanes; user lingering is enabled. The one-minute fleet
monitor and the dedicated `ipfs-taskboard-legal-ir-watchdog.service` and
`ipfs-taskboard-legal-ir-repair.service` remain the existing operational owners.
Their scoped configuration is
`~/.config/ipfs-taskboard-watchdog/legal-ir/fleet.json`; status is under
`~/.local/state/ipfs-taskboard-watchdog/legal-ir/`. No second watchdog or task
board was installed.

The loop polls every 60 seconds, requires fleet evidence no older than 180
seconds, recognizes stalls after 900 seconds and retains the configured
failure/blocked grace periods, cooldown, bounded retries, backoff and operator
holds. Real task progress and admitted work determine recovery. A completed
engineering board with no runnable tasks is healthy idle; missing reviewed
source meaning, context or dependencies remains an explicit scientific block.
Recovery cannot invent legal decisions, turn fixtures into gold or confer task
completion authority.

A scoped cancellation bug was fixed: dependency-wait recovery now targets the
configured LegalIR repair-job unit instead of the unrelated global default.
The active source generation passed 21 focused cases; the separate canonical
Accelerate backport passed 33 cases. These cohorts are not pooled. Broader
blocked-network-profile runs retained the same 19 baseline failures and remain
unqualified; all earlier failed tests and deployment preparations are retained.
Only the two auxiliary daemons were reloaded. All four supervisor lane PIDs
remained unchanged, and the observed engineering population stayed 135 completed
tasks with no ready work. This is not completion of the 24 programme goals.

The deployed import path keeps the qualified source checkout first and the
sealed runtime archive second, preserving deferred provider adapters. Source
discovery imported one local compatibility adapter but made no provider process
or model calls; it is not a new provider qualification. The watchdog repair
dispatcher remains available for future bounded technical incidents. Task
materialization and current owner/claim/resource admission are still required
before any child preview becomes runnable.

### Evaluation, release and immediate queue

Freeze sample counts, source-group exclusions and acceptance criteria before fitting. Group repeated spans, common source occurrences, interpretation alternatives and shared definition families to prevent leakage; record dependency sharing and use a stricter disconnected-component cohort for claims beyond known context. Already exposed wording/development sets remain exposed. Report each distinct group once, physical repetitions separately, all enrolled failures/refusals, and uncertainty appropriate to the population size.

| Measure | Required observation |
| --- | --- |
| Vector reconstruction | MSE/cosine against the exact native source representation; identify identity projections and bypasses. This measures vectors, separately from legal reconstruction. |
| Token training | Teacher-forced CE/perplexity under the declared codec and objective; reference-prefix access is training/diagnostic information. |
| Autonomous semantic IR | Complete exact canonical typed AST from BOS, completion and abstention over all enrolled sources; ordered operands/binders survive declared normalization. |
| IR to legal prose | Generate from the declared IR inputs without the expected source text; report normalized-text exactness and independently reviewed meaning fidelity separately. |
| Original text recovery | Exact UTF-8 byte equality, distinguishing source-free generation from source-map/retained-surface restoration. Report the supplied information and route for every score. |
| Legal structure | Separate modality/polarity, ordered roles, binder count/scope, conditions/exceptions, definition binding and temporal correctness against reviewed targets. |
| Graph | Exact provenance and AST round-trip; scoped sense/entity/link accuracy; dependency closure, alternative and unresolved coverage. |
| Lean | Actual pinned project build and complete receipt; supported AST preservation, theorem lineage/axiom policy, non-vacuity, negative controls and invalidation. Explicit legal assumptions remain assumptions. |
| Practical cost | Actual encoder/hash/cache I/O, decoder steps/forwards/updates, memory, wall time, review effort, Lean work and complete resource closeout. Nested intervals are not additive speedup evidence. |
| Supervisor context cost | Measure actual transmitted tokens and retrieval/cache misses for the same task/accepted result. Reuse a versioned capsule of selected source/definition/graph/proof references with independent inference permissions. Vector width and minified prose alone do not establish token savings or preserved task satisfaction. |

Use an explicit enrollment funnel: `N_enrolled`, `N_reviewed`, `N_representable`, `N_attempted`, `N_complete`, `N_exact`, `N_meaning_reviewed`, `N_graph_closed` and `N_kernel_checked`. Report exactness over reviewed representable targets and exact-output yield over all enrolled sources; the latter measures coverage, not accuracy for unknown labels. Enumerate unsupported grammar, OOV, budget overflow, abstentions, incomplete outputs and unknown labels separately; unknown labels remain unscored rather than silently excluded. Require lossless codec roundtrip for accepted targets and zero silent truncation. Freeze thresholds and the selector before fitting; a small integration component is not a population-level quality estimate.

Convergence acceptance belongs to LIR-19/23: predeclare the optimization algorithm, data/seed groups, fixed update and resource budget, checkpoint selector, and semantic/generalization thresholds before fitting. Retain complete learning curves and autonomous outputs at fixed checkpoints; report stability across independent seeds on a larger holdout after the small feasibility panel. A falling loss or a finite plateau is bounded empirical evidence. A mathematical convergence claim requires a separate explicit objective, assumptions and theorem; it does not prove statutory fidelity. Lean verifies the emitted formal claims under their recorded assumptions, while independent source/meaning review establishes whether those claims express the selected law.

A semantic AST can represent several different wordings. Guaranteed original-byte restoration therefore requires retained lossless surface information, source maps or the original source object. Keep that archival route available for the user's legal text→LegalIR→legal text requirement, while measuring learned source-free text generation separately. A pointer/source-text-conditioned decoder must also report its source-only and latent-disabled baselines: successful copying does not establish that its vector retained the law. Every benchmark record must declare actual inputs, target/task, codec, output cap, exposure group and all enrolled failures/refusals.

### Resource and reuse defaults for the implementation plan

Execution must use the existing native/resource owners. Queue supported compiler/decompiler repair work through `autoformal.supervisor_queue` into the accelerate supervisor's `DatabaseTaskSource`; inspect the selected supervisor's supported interface for other orchestration. Direct bounded native qualification retains its existing execution owner. Pin the actual policy and declared per-task memory, CPU, process, GPU and storage limits; keep runtime admission enabled. Use existing cache and model-session owners, bounded batches and queues, and shared resident models where their lifecycle supports it. Parallelize independent source/graph components and independent build dependencies only within admitted capacity. Plan validation of outside-load backoff, bounded cancellation and resumable progress through these owners; do not increase capacity or relax headroom to make a campaign start.

If a provider is needed, route through the existing provider adapter and supervisor retry policy. Require finite retries, `Retry-After` handling, jittered backoff and a wall-clock/cost budget; preserve rate-limited, timeout, cancelled and not-started work separately from model failures. Verify these behaviors for the selected route before a larger run. A cache hit must match source text/context, producer, model/checkpoint, codec and governing dependency identities; vector width or a matching term string alone cannot authorize reuse.

Keep implementation and maintained documentation in the external owners above. Update this handoff, the existing work catalog and the same24-task backlog after receipt-backed progress. Original artifacts remain immutable evidence with their existing locators; no new dated implementation tree, registry or parallel supervisor is needed.
